2026 IronPAX Leaderboard

Four-week cumulative individual standings and participation-weighted regional standings.

Scoring rules

Each individual is identified by the pair (f3Name, region). For each missing weekly leaderboard appearance, the participant receives a weekly rank of 10,000. Lower cumulative scores rank higher.

Individual score = Week 1 rank + Week 2 rank + Week 3 rank + Week 4 rank
Top-250 regional points = 251 − overall individual place
Participation ratio = completionRate / 100
Regional weighted score = sum(top-250 regional points) × participation ratio

Regional standings

Only regions with at least one individual in the overall top 250 are shown. Regional standings sort by highest participation-weighted score.
